jabesh-gilead runs the sentence; we do not punish by inference; encamp outside the camp seven days and purify yourselves on the third and the seventh day, you and your captives; every garment, skin, goat-work and wood; eleazar: this is the statute of the torah — the gold, silver, bronze, iron, tin and lead through the fire and the water of sprinkling, the rest through water; wash on the seventh day and afterward come into the camp — the heifer's law in the priest's mouth; take the sum of the prey — you, eleazar and the heads of the fathers; halve it between those who took the war and the congregation; levy a tribute to the lord from the warriors' half, one soul of the five hundred, to eleazar the lord's heave-offering; from israel's half one held of the fifty to the levites who keep the charge of the tabernacle; and they did as the lord commanded — the half-shekel's spelling and phrase, the fraction the parser leaves silent, the shelf silent from here;
